        bool GetMayFireAgain();
        [System.Obsolete("Use the NextFireTimeUtc property instead.")]
        System.DateTimeOffset? GetNextFireTimeUtc();
        [System.Obsolete("Use the PreviousFireTimeUtc property instead.")]
        System.DateTimeOffset? GetPreviousFireTimeUtc();
        Quartz.IScheduleBuilder GetScheduleBuilder();
        Quartz.TriggerBuilder GetTriggerBuilder();
    }

        public SystemTextJsonObjectSerializer() { }
        public SystemTextJsonObjectSerializer(Quartz.Serialization.Json.SystemTextJsonSerializerRegistry registry) { }
        protected Quartz.Serialization.Json.SystemTextJsonSerializerRegistry Registry { get; }
        protected virtual System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions CreateSerializerOptions() { }
        public T? Deserialize<T>(byte[] data)
            where T :  class { }
        public byte[] Serialize<T>(T obj)
            where T :  class { }
    }
    public abstract class TaskSchedulingThreadPool : Quartz.Extensibility.IThreadPool
    {
        protected const int DefaultMaxConcurrency = 10;
        protected TaskSchedulingThreadPool() { }
        protected TaskSchedulingThreadPool(int maxConcurrency) { }
        public int MaxConcurrency { get; set; }
        public virtual int PoolSize { get; }
        public System.Threading.Tasks.TaskScheduler Scheduler { get; set; }
        public int ThreadCount { get; set; }
        protected abstract System.Threading.Tasks.TaskScheduler GetDefaultScheduler();
        public virtual System.Threading.Tasks.ValueTask Initialize(System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken = default) { }
        public System.Threading.Tasks.ValueTask Shutdown(bool waitForJobsToComplete = true, System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken = default) { }
        public System.Threading.Tasks.ValueTask<bool> TryRun(System.Func<System.Threading.Tasks.Task> action, System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken = default) { }
        public System.Threading.Tasks.ValueTask<int> WaitForAvailableThreads(System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken = default) { }
    }
    public sealed class ZeroSizeThreadPool : Quartz.Extensibility.IThreadPool
    {
        public ZeroSizeThreadPool() { }
        public int PoolSize { get; }
        public System.Threading.Tasks.ValueTask Initialize(System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken = default) { }
        public System.Threading.Tasks.ValueTask Shutdown(bool waitForJobsToComplete = true, System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken = default) { }

        public System.Threading.Tasks.ValueTask<int> WaitForAvailableThreads(System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken = default) { }
    }

        where TKey : Quartz.Util.Key<TKey>
    {

        public Quartz.IMatcher<TKey> RightOperand { get; }
        public override bool Equals(object? obj) { }
        public override int GetHashCode() { }
        public bool IsMatch(TKey key) { }
        public static Quartz.Impl.Matchers.AndMatcher<T> And<T>(Quartz.IMatcher<T> leftOperand, Quartz.IMatcher<T> rightOperand)
            where T : Quartz.Util.Key<T> { }
    }

    }

    public sealed class OrMatcher<TKey> : Quartz.IMatcher<TKey>
        where TKey : Quartz.Util.Key<TKey>

        public Quartz.IMatcher<TKey> RightOperand { get; }

    }
    [System.Serializable]
    public abstract class StringMatcher<TKey> : Quartz.IMatcher<TKey>
        where TKey : Quartz.Util.Key<TKey>
    {
        protected StringMatcher(string compareTo, Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator compareWith) { }
        public string CompareToValue { get; }
        public Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator CompareWithOperator { get; }
        public override bool Equals(object? obj) { }
        public override int GetHashCode() { }
        protected abstract string GetValue(TKey key);
        public bool IsMatch(TKey key) { }
        public override string ToString() { }
    }
    [System.Serializable]
    public abstract class StringOperator : System.IEquatable<Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator>
    {
        public static readonly Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator Anything;
        public static readonly Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator Contains;
        public static readonly Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator EndsWith;
        public static readonly Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator Equality;
        public static readonly Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator StartsWith;
        protected StringOperator() { }
        public virtual bool Equals(Quartz.Impl.Matchers.StringOperator? other) { }
        public override bool Equals(object? obj) { }
        public abstract bool Evaluate(string value, string compareTo);
        public override int GetHashCode() { }
    }

    [System.Serializable]
    public abstract class AbstractTrigger : Quartz.Extensibility.IMutableTrigger, Quartz.Extensibility.IOperableTrigger, Quartz.ITrigger, System.IEquatable<Quartz.Impl.Triggers.AbstractTrigger>
    {
        protected AbstractTrigger() { }
        protected AbstractTrigger(System.TimeProvider timeProvider) { }
        protected AbstractTrigger(string name, System.TimeProvider timeProvider) { }
        protected AbstractTrigger(string name, string group, System.TimeProvider timeProvider) { }
        protected AbstractTrigger(string name, string group, string jobName, string jobGroup, System.TimeProvider timeProvider) { }
        public virtual string? CalendarName { get; set; }
        public virtual string? Description { get; set; }
        public virtual System.DateTimeOffset? EndTimeUtc { get; set; }
        public string? ExecutionGroup { get; set; }
        public abstract System.DateTimeOffset? FinalFireTimeUtc { get; }
        public virtual string FireInstanceId { get; set; }
        public virtual bool HasAdditionalProperties { get; }
        public abstract bool HasMillisecondPrecision { get; }
        public bool IsPreferredNodeAuto { get; }
        public virtual Quartz.JobDataMap JobDataMap { get; set; }
        public Quartz.JobKey JobKey { get; set; }
        public Quartz.TriggerKey Key { get; set; }
        public virtual int MisfireInstruction { get; set; }
        public abstract System.DateTimeOffset? NextFireTimeUtc { get; set; }
        public string? PreferredNode { get; set; }
        public abstract System.DateTimeOffset? PreviousFireTimeUtc { get; set; }
        public virtual int Priority { get; set; }
        public virtual System.DateTimeOffset StartTimeUtc { get; set; }
        public virtual Quartz.ITrigger Clone() { }
        public abstract System.DateTimeOffset? ComputeFirstFireTimeUtc(Quartz.ICalendar? calendar);
        public virtual bool Equals(Quartz.Impl.Triggers.AbstractTrigger? trigger) { }
        public override bool Equals(object? obj) { }
        public virtual Quartz.SchedulerInstruction ExecutionComplete(Quartz.IJobExecutionContext context, Quartz.JobExecutionException? result) { }
        public abstract System.DateTimeOffset? GetFireTimeAfter(System.DateTimeOffset? afterTime);
        public override int GetHashCode() { }
        public abstract bool GetMayFireAgain();
        [System.Obsolete("Use the NextFireTimeUtc property instead.")]
        public System.DateTimeOffset? GetNextFireTimeUtc() { }
        [System.Obsolete("Use the PreviousFireTimeUtc property instead.")]
        public System.DateTimeOffset? GetPreviousFireTimeUtc() { }
        public abstract Quartz.IScheduleBuilder GetScheduleBuilder();
        public Quartz.TriggerBuilder GetTriggerBuilder() { }
        public override string ToString() { }
        public abstract void Triggered(Quartz.ICalendar? calendar);
        public abstract void UpdateAfterMisfire(Quartz.ICalendar? calendar);
        public abstract void UpdateWithNewCalendar(Quartz.ICalendar calendar, System.TimeSpan misfireThreshold);
        public virtual void Validate() { }
        protected abstract bool ValidateMisfireInstruction(int misfireInstruction);
    }
